- **divorce-practice-stephane-boghossian**: 离婚与家事律师的 AI 协同律师——一个跨司法辖区可移植、覆盖完整案件生命周期的脚手架。八种操作模式映射案件的实际推进方式：受理与入职、财务披露、子女与抚养、财产分割与 QDRO（合格国内关系令）、证据开示与文件审查、起草、谈判与调解准备、出庭准备——外加判决后变更与执行。方法论与司法辖区无关：它强制对每份当地表格、公式或规则进行真实、可核实的研究，而非臆造，将普通法、共同财产制、大陆法和中东北非（MENA）属人法制度作为变量处理。围绕一条不可谈判的原则构建：特权（privilege）。它负责起草、分析、组织和压力测试。它不是律师本身。 ([CSlawyer1985/legal-skillhub](https://github.com/CSlawyer1985/legal-skillhub/tree/HEAD/skills/divorce-practice-stephane-boghossian) · ★37)

- **divvy**: 들어온 일을 두 러너(Claude Code · Codex CLI)에 배정하고, Codex 몫은 실제로 `codex exec`로 던져 결과를 회수하는 분배기. primary는 도구 게이트 → 브리프 비용 → 적성(ROSTER)으로 고르고, 오판 비용은 별도로 검토자를 붙인다(검토자는 항상 primary가 아닌 러너). "분배해줘", "이건 누가 풀어", "누가 잘 풀지", "코덱스한테 돌려", "코덱스로 넘겨", "divvy", "이 작업들 나눠줘", "dispatch", "누구한테 시킬까" 요청에 트리거. 티어·추론량 판정(모델 중립)은 `modelchk`, 산출물 교차리뷰 루프는 `peer-review`가 정본 — 이 스킬은 **누가 할지**만 정하고 실행을 붙인다. ([kaidomo/divvy-skill](https://github.com/kaidomo/divvy-skill/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md) · ★1)
- **divvy-up**: Run an approved implementation plan as waves of parallel subagents whose tasks own disjoint files. It never writes the plan: to plan, use writing-plans. ([kendrick/skills](https://github.com/kendrick/skills/tree/HEAD/divvy-up))
- **diw-ci-manola**: The Manola corporate identity as law — the page is white, text is black, lime is the brand, violet is the accent. Use for "/diw-use-customer-identity &lt;customer&gt;", "use the manola CI", "on-brand", "which token", "is this on brand", "brand review", "apply the CI", "build a CI skill", "the brand says", or whenever a named brand and a design question… ([yesitsfebreeze/goodesign](https://github.com/yesitsfebreeze/goodesign/tree/HEAD/skills/diw-use-customer-identity))

- **dixieme-homme**: Builds the complete opposing thesis against a consensus — pleads the inverse position as if it were true, all the way through. Triggers on "play devil's advocate", "argue the opposite case", "what if we're wrong", "build the other side's case", "take apart this consensus".
